## Sheet: Table of Contents
| NSW Fire extent and severity mapping (FESM) results for the 2016-17 fire year |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| This spreadsheet reports on the fire extent and severity of wildfires >100 hectares (ha) that occurred in the 2016-17 fire year. Fires are processed through the semi-automated system built between NSW Rural Fire Service and DPIE Remote Sensing & Regulatory Mapping agencies.  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| The accompanying Fire extent and severity mapping annual report is available (add hyperlink when live) |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Figures generated within each FESM report may be updated in future reports to reflect improved processing methods, data availability or changed administrative boundaries. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| This workbook contains the following data: |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
